# 1. Create the access directory if it doesn't already exist
mkdir -p /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access

# 2. Append the LLDAP realm configuration directly to domains.cfg
### Note: Indentations below the 'ldap: LLDAP-Domain' line MUST use a single Tab or spaces.
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

# ==================== GLOBAL CONFIGURATION ====================
REALM="LLDAP-Domain"
LDAP_SERVER="192.168.110.232"
LDAP_PORT="3890"
BASE_DN="dc=example,dc=com"
BIND_DN="uid=admin,ou=people,dc=example,dc=com"
# ==============================================================

# 1. Install prerequisites (ldap-utils) if missing
if ! command -v ldapsearch &> /dev/null; then
    echo "📦 Installing ldap-utils..."
    apt-get update && apt-get install -y ldap-utils
fi

# 2. Ensure PDM configuration directories exist
mkdir -p /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access

# 3. Single centralized password prompt
read -sp "Please enter the LLDAP admin password: " LLDAP_PASS
echo ""

# 4. Generate the PDM Realm configuration dynamically using variables
#Remove old realms
>  /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/domains.cfg

# Using printf with '\t' ensures strict internal hardware tabs are preserved
printf "ldap: %s
\tbase-dn %s
\tserver1 %s
\tport %s
\tuser-attr uid
\tcomment LLDAP Infrastructure Domain
\tbind-dn %s
\tuser-classes person
\tsync-defaults-options enable-new=1
" "${REALM}" "${BASE_DN}" "${LDAP_SERVER}" "${LDAP_PORT}" "${BIND_DN}" > /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/domains.cfg

echo "✅ Domains configuration generated dynamically."

# 5. Securely inject password into PDM's isolated JSON store
export LLDAP_PASS
python3 -c "
import json, os
file_path = '/etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/ldap-passwords.json'
data = json.load(open(file_path)) if os.path.exists(file_path) else {}
data['${REALM}'] = os.getenv('LLDAP_PASS')
with open(file_path, 'w') as f:
    json.dump(data, f, indent=4)
"
chmod 600 /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/ldap-passwords.json
echo "✅ LLDAP admin bind password successfully stored in PDM vault."

# 6. Helper function to query LLDAP for group memberships
get_group_members() {
    local group_cn=$1
    ldapsearch -H "ldap://${LDAP_SERVER}:${LDAP_PORT}" \
        -x -D "${BIND_DN}" -w "${LLDAP_PASS}" \
        -b "${BASE_DN}" \
        "(&(objectClass=person)(memberOf=cn=${group_cn},ou=groups,${BASE_DN}))" uid | \
        grep "^uid:" | awk '{print $2}'
}

echo "🔄 Fetching current group memberships from LLDAP..."
SUPER_ADMINS=$(get_group_members "lldap_superAdministrator")
ADMINS=$(get_group_members "lldap_admin")
READONLY=$(get_group_members "lldap_strict_readonly")
PASSADMIN=$(get_group_members "lldap_password_manager")
NONTECH=$(get_group_members "lldap_nonTechnicalUser")

# Wipe environment password string for runtime privacy
unset LLDAP_PASS

# 7. Initialize a clean, pristine acl.cfg file
> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg
echo "✍️ Processing assignments for PDM..."

# Map Super Admins & Admins to the Administrator role (root equivalent)
for user in $SUPER_ADMINS $ADMINS; do
    if [ -n "$user" ]; then
        echo "acl:1:/:${user}@${REALM}:Administrator" >> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg
        echo "  -> Granted Administrator to: ${user}@${REALM}"
    fi
done

# Map Read-only, password managers, and non-technical users to the Auditor role
for user in $READONLY $PASSADMIN $NONTECH; do
    if [ -n "$user" ]; then
        echo "acl:1:/:${user}@${REALM}:Auditor" >> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg
        echo "  -> Granted Auditor to: ${user}@${REALM}"
    fi
done

# 8. Restart the API engine daemon to instantly activate configuration mappings
systemctl restart proxmox-datacenter-api.service
echo "🚀 Sync complete! PDM access controls match your LLDAP groups perfectly."
```


# Create an auto-run script to keep the PDM server synced with LLDAP

```bash
cat << 'EOF' > /usr/local/bin/pdm-security-sync.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

# ==================== SECURE CREDENTIALS CONFIGURATION ====================
REALM="LLDAP-Domain"
LDAP_SERVER="192.168.110.232"
LDAP_PORT="3890"
BASE_DN="dc=example,dc=com"
BIND_DN="uid=admin,ou=people,dc=example,dc=com"

# Fill these out so the script can run autonomously at midnight via cron
LLDAP_PASS="Lempx64X"
PDM_ROOT_PASS="Lempx64X"
# ==========================================================================

# 1. Ensure prerequisites (ldap-utils) are present
if ! command -v ldapsearch &> /dev/null; then
    apt-get update && apt-get install -y ldap-utils > /dev/null 2>&1
fi

# 2. TRIGGER DIRECT PDM REALM USER SYNC FIRST
# This forces PDM to poll LLDAP and create user accounts internally
TEMP_PASS_FILE=$(mktemp)
chmod 600 "$TEMP_PASS_FILE"
echo "$PDM_ROOT_PASS" > "$TEMP_PASS_FILE"

if ! proxmox-datacenter-manager-client access domains sync "${REALM}" \
    --host "localhost" \
    --user "root@pam" \
    --password-file "$TEMP_PASS_FILE" \
    --fingerprint "ignore" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    
    # Fallback endpoint check for varying API engine revisions
    proxmox-datacenter-manager-client access domains update "${REALM}" \
        --host "localhost" \
        --user "root@pam" \
        --password-file "$TEMP_PASS_FILE" \
        --fingerprint "ignore" \
        --sync true > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
fi

rm -f "$TEMP_PASS_FILE"

# 3. FETCH CURRENT GROUP MEMBERSHIPS FROM LLDAP
get_group_members() {
    local group_cn=$1
    ldapsearch -H "ldap://${LDAP_SERVER}:${LDAP_PORT}" \
        -x -D "${BIND_DN}" -w "${LLDAP_PASS}" \
        -b "${BASE_DN}" \
        "(&(objectClass=person)(memberOf=cn=${group_cn},ou=groups,${BASE_DN}))" uid | \
        grep "^uid:" | awk '{print $2}'
}

SUPER_ADMINS=$(get_group_members "lldap_superAdministrator")
ADMINS=$(get_group_members "lldap_admin")
READONLY=$(get_group_members "lldap_strict_readonly")
PASSADMIN=$(get_group_members "lldap_password_manager")
NONTECH=$(get_group_members "lldap_nonTechnicalUser")

# 4. REBUILD THE ACL MAP FROM SCRATCH (True State Syncing)
mkdir -p /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access
> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg

# Bind Super Admins & Admins to the Administrator role
for user in $SUPER_ADMINS $ADMINS; do
    if [ -n "$user" ]; then
        echo "acl:1:/:${user}@${REALM}:Administrator" >> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg
    fi
done

# Bind Read-only, password managers, and non-technical users to the Auditor role
for user in $READONLY $PASSADMIN $NONTECH; do
    if [ -n "$user" ]; then
        echo "acl:1:/:${user}@${REALM}:Auditor" >> /etc/proxmox-datacenter-manager/access/acl.cfg
    fi
done

# 5. RESTART DAEMON TO COMMIT PERMISSIONS
systemctl restart proxmox-datacenter-api.service
echo "[$(date)] ✅ PDM Users & Group Permissions matching LLDAP successfully synchronized."
EOF

chmod 700 /usr/local/bin/pdm-security-sync.sh

# Update packages and install the cron daemon
apt-get update && apt-get install -y cron

# Enable and start the cron service
systemctl enable --now cron

echo "0 0 * * * root /usr/local/bin/pdm-security-sync.sh >> /var/log/pdm-ldap-sync.log 2>&1" > /etc/cron.d/pdm-security-sync

cat /etc/cron.d/pdm-security-sync

```

# Set community scripts and remove subscription

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

echo "⚠️ Transitioning PDM Server to Community No-Subscription Repositories..."

# 1. Dynamically target the correct Debian release codename (Bookworm / Trixie)
CODENAME=$(awk -F= '/^VERSION_CODENAME=/ {print $2}' /etc/os-release)
if [ -z "$CODENAME" ]; then
    CODENAME=$(lsb_release -sc 2>/dev/null || echo "trixie")
fi

# 2. Handle Repository conversion based on configuration formats
# Check and adapt for modern DEB822 (.sources) layout format
if [ -f "/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-enterprise.sources" ]; then
    echo "⚙️ DEB822 configuration syntax found. Adjusting source definitions..."
    sed -i 's/^Enabled: true/Enabled: false/g' /etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-enterprise.sources
    
    cat <<EOF > /etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-no-subscription.sources
Types: deb
URIs: http://download.proxmox.com/debian/pdm
Suites: $CODENAME
Components: pdm-no-subscription
Signed-By: /usr/share/keyrings/proxmox-archive-keyring.gpg
EOF

# Fallback check and adapt for traditional style (.list) files
else
    echo "⚙️ Traditional list layout found. Modifying target configuration files..."
    if [ -f "/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-enterprise.list" ]; then
        sed -i 's/^deb/# deb/g' /etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-enterprise.list
    fi
    
    echo "deb http://download.proxmox.com/debian/pdm $CODENAME pdm-no-subscription" > /etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-no-subscription.list
fi

echo "✅ Package streams successfully pointed to Proxmox Community mirrors."

# 3. Patch the Shared UI Library to bypass the subscription nag mechanism
echo "🛠️ Modifying shared widget toolkit framework assets..."
WIDGET_TOOLKIT_LIB="/usr/share/javascript/proxmox-widget-toolkit/proxmoxlib.js"

if [ -f "$WIDGET_TOOLKIT_LIB" ]; then
    # Create an emergency rollback backup clone of the asset file
    cp "$WIDGET_TOOLKIT_LIB" "${WIDGET_TOOLKIT_LIB}.bak"
    
    # Inject a leading void instruction to inline-comment out the popup generator function execution loop
    sed -Ezi "s/(Ext.Msg.show\(\{\s+title: gettext\('No valid sub)/void\(\{ \/\/\1/g" "$WIDGET_TOOLKIT_LIB"
    echo "✅ UI subscription logic intercept successfully patched."
else
    echo "⚠️ Target framework file path absent. Skipping frontend interface patch."
fi

# 4. Refresh internal apt indices and restart web dashboard daemon 
echo "🔄 Synchronizing local package databases and clearing application caches..."
apt-get update

systemctl restart proxmox-datacenter-api.service

echo "🚀 Execution completed successfully! Community feeds are armed and the verification prompt has been bypassed."
echo "💡 IMPORTANT: You must completely clear your web browser cache (or press Ctrl + F5) for the patch to show up in the web GUI."

```

\"$APP\"\nvariables\ncolor\ncatch_errors\n\nfunction update_script() {\n  header_info\n  check_container_storage\n  check_container_resources\n  if [[ ! -e /usr/sbin/proxmox-datacenter-manager-admin ]]; then\n    msg_error \"No ${APP} Installation Found!\"\n    exit\n  fi\n\n  if grep -q 'Debian GNU/Linux 12' /etc/os-release && [ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/proxmox-release-bookworm.list ] && [ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-test.list ]; then\n    msg_info \"Updating outdated outdated source formats\"\n    echo \"deb [signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/proxmox-archive-keyring.gpg] http://download.proxmox.com/debian/pdm bookworm pdm-test\" >/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-test.list\n    curl -fsSL https://enterprise.proxmox.com/debian/proxmox-archive-keyring-trixie.gpg -o /usr/share/keyrings/proxmox-archive-keyring.gpg\n    rm -f /etc/apt/keyrings/proxmox-release-bookworm.gpg /etc/apt/sources.list.d/proxmox-release-bookworm.list\n    $STD apt update\n    msg_ok \"Updated old sources\"\n  fi\n\n  if grep -q 'Debian GNU/Linux 13' /etc/os-release; then\n    if [ -f \"/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-test.sources\" ]; then\n      if ! grep -qx \"Enabled: false\" \"/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-test.sources\"; then\n          echo \"Enabled: false\" >> \"/etc/apt/sources.list.d/pdm-test.sources\"\n          setup_deb822_repo \\\n            \"pdm\" \\\n            \"https://enterprise.proxmox.com/debian/proxmox-archive-keyring-trixie.gpg\" \\\n            \"http://download.proxmox.com/debian/pdm\" \\\n            \"trixie\" \\\n            \"pdm-no-subscription\"\n      fi\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  msg_info \"Updating $APP LXC\"\n  $STD apt update\n  $STD apt -y upgrade\n  msg_ok \"Updated $APP LXC\"\n  msg_ok \"Updated successfully!\"\n  exit\n}\n\nstart\nbuild_container\ndescription\n\nmsg_ok \"Completed successfully!\\n\"\necho -e \"${CREATING}${GN}${APP} setup has been successfully initialized!${CL}\"\necho -e \"${INFO}${YW}Access It handles:\n#\n#   - Variable initialization and defaults\n#   - Container creation and resource allocation\n#   - Storage selection and management\n#   - Advanced configuration and customization\n#   - User interaction menus and prompts\n#\n# Usage:\n#   - Sourced automatically by CT creation scripts\n#   - Requires core.func and error_handler.func to be loaded first\n#\n# Initializes core variables for container creation\n# - Normalizes application name (NSAPP = lowercase, no spaces)\n# - Builds installer filename (var_install)\n# - Defines regex patterns for validation\n# - Fetches Proxmox hostname and version\n# - Generates unique session ID for tracking and logging\n# - Captures app-declared resource defaults (CPU, RAM, Disk)\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nvariables() {\n  NSAPP=$(echo \"${APP,,}\" | tr -d ' ')              # This function sets the NSAPP variable by converting the value of the APP variable to lowercase and removing any spaces.\n  var_install=\"${NSAPP}-install\"                    # sets the var_install variable by appending \"-install\" to the value of NSAPP.\n  INTEGER='^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$'                    # it defines the INTEGER regular expression pattern.\n  PVEHOST_NAME=$(hostname)                          # gets the Proxmox Hostname and sets it to Uppercase\n  DIAGNOSTICS=\"no\"                                  # Safe default: no telemetry until user consents via diagnostics_check()\n  METHOD=\"default\"                                  # sets the METHOD variable to \"default\", used for the API call.\n  RANDOM_UUID=\"$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid)\" # generates a random UUID and sets it to the RANDOM_UUID variable.\n  EXECUTION_ID=\"${RANDOM_UUID}\"                     # Unique execution ID for telemetry record identification (unique-indexed in PocketBase)\n  SESSION_ID=\"${RANDOM_UUID:0:8}\"                   # Short session ID (first 8 chars of UUID) for log files\n  BUILD_LOG=\"/tmp/create-lxc-${SESSION_ID}.log\"     # Host-side container creation log\n  # NOTE: combined_log is constructed locally in build_container() and ensure_log_on_host()\n  # as \"/tmp/${NSAPP}-${CTID}-${SESSION_ID}.log\" (requires CTID, not available here)\n  CTTYPE=\"${CTTYPE:-${CT_TYPE:-1}}\"\n\n  # ARM64 Template default variables\n  DEBIAN_DEFAULT_CODENAME=\"trixie\"\n  UBUNTU_DEFAULT_CODENAME=\"noble\"\n  ALPINE_DEFAULT_VERSION=\"3.23\"\n\n  # Parse dev_mode early\n  parse_dev_mode\n\n  # Setup persistent log directory if logs mode active\n  if [[ \"${DEV_MODE_LOGS:-false}\" == \"true\" ]]; then\n    mkdir -p /var/log/community-scripts\n    BUILD_LOG=\"/var/log/community-scripts/create-lxc-${SESSION_ID}-$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).log\"\n  fi\n\n  # Get Proxmox VE version and kernel version\n  if command -v pveversion >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    PVEVERSION=\"$(pveversion | awk -F'/' '{print $2}' | awk -F'-' '{print $1}')\"\n  else\n    PVEVERSION=\"N/A\"\n  fi\n  KERNEL_VERSION=$(uname -r)\n\n  # Capture app-declared defaults (for precedence logic)\n  # These values are set by the app script BEFORE default.vars is loaded\n  # If app declares higher values than default.vars, app values take precedence\n  if [[ -n \"${var_cpu:-}\" && \"${var_cpu}\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n    export APP_DEFAULT_CPU=\"${var_cpu}\"\n  fi\n  if [[ -n \"${var_ram:-}\" && \"${var_ram}\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n    export APP_DEFAULT_RAM=\"${var_ram}\"\n  fi\n  if [[ -n \"${var_disk:-}\" && \"${var_disk}\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n    export APP_DEFAULT_DISK=\"${var_disk}\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# `source <(curl ...)` hides the download exit code, so a failed fetch would\n# silently source an empty stream and leave the helpers undefined.\n_bootstrap_die() {\n  if declare -f msg_error >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    msg_error \"$1\"\n  else\n    echo \"FATAL: $1\" >&2\n  fi\n  exit 115\n}\n\n_bootstrap_source() {\n  local url=\"$1\" probe=\"$2\" content=\"\"\n  if command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    content=$(curl -fsSL --connect-timeout 10 --retry 5 --retry-connrefused --retry-delay 2 \"$url\") || content=\"\"\n  elif command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    content=$(wget -qO- --tries=5 --timeout=10 \"$url\") || content=\"\"\n  else\n    _bootstrap_die \"Neither curl nor wget available\"\n  fi\n  [[ -n \"$content\" ]] || _bootstrap_die \"Failed to download ${url##*/}\"\n  source /dev/stdin <<<\"$content\"\n  declare -f \"$probe\" >/dev/null 2>&1 ||\n    _bootstrap_die \"${url##*/} loaded but incomplete (missing ${probe})\"\n}\n\n_FUNC_BASE=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/community-scripts/ProxmoxVE/main/misc\"\n_bootstrap_source \"$_FUNC_BASE/api.func\" post_to_api\n_bootstrap_source \"$_FUNC_BASE/core.func\" load_functions\n_bootstrap_source \"$_FUNC_BASE/error_handler.func\" catch_errors\nload_functions\ncatch_errors\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# SECTION 2: PRE-FLIGHT CHECKS & SYSTEM VALIDATION\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# maxkeys_check()\n#\n# - Reads kernel keyring limits (maxkeys, maxbytes)\n# - Checks current usage for LXC user (UID 100000)\n# - Warns if usage is close to limits and suggests sysctl tuning\n# - Exits if thresholds are exceeded\n# - https://cleveruptime.com/docs/files/proc-key-users | https://docs.kernel.org/security/keys/core.html\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmaxkeys_check() {\n  # Read kernel parameters\n  per_user_maxkeys=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/keys/maxkeys 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  per_user_maxbytes=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/keys/maxbytes 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n\n  # Exit if kernel parameters are unavailable\n  if [[ \"$per_user_maxkeys\" -eq 0 || \"$per_user_maxbytes\" -eq 0 ]]; then\n    msg_error \"Unable to read kernel key parameters. Ensure proper permissions.\"\n    exit 107\n  fi\n\n  # Fetch key usage for user ID 100000 (typical for containers)\n  used_lxc_keys=$(awk '/100000:/ {print $2}' /proc/key-users 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  used_lxc_bytes=$(awk '/100000:/ {split($5, a, \"/\"); print a[1]}' /proc/key-users 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n\n  # Calculate thresholds and suggested new limits\n  threshold_keys=$((per_user_maxkeys - 100))\n  threshold_bytes=$((per_user_maxbytes - 1000))\n  new_limit_keys=$((per_user_maxkeys * 2))\n  new_limit_bytes=$((per_user_maxbytes * 2))\n\n  # Check if key or byte usage is near limits\n  failure=0\n  if [[ \"$used_lxc_keys\" -gt \"$threshold_keys\" ]]; then\n    msg_warn \"Key usage is near the limit (${used_lxc_keys}/${per_user_maxkeys})\"\n    echo -e \"${INFO} Suggested action: Set ${GN}kernel.keys.maxkeys=${new_limit_keys}${CL} in ${BOLD}/etc/sysctl.d/98-community-scripts.conf${CL}.\"\n    failure=1\n  fi\n  if [[ \"$used_lxc_bytes\" -gt \"$threshold_bytes\" ]]; then\n    msg_warn \"Key byte usage is near the limit (${used_lxc_bytes}/${per_user_maxbytes})\"\n    echo -e \"${INFO} Suggested action: Set ${GN}kernel.keys.maxbytes=${new_limit_bytes}${CL} in ${BOLD}/etc/sysctl.d/98-community-scripts.conf${CL}.\"\n    failure=1\n  fi\n\n  # Provide next steps if issues are detected\n  if [[ \"$failure\" -eq 1 ]]; then\n    msg_error \"Kernel key limits exceeded - see s\n\n### json/proxmox-datacenter-manager.json 404\n404: Not Found", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

\"Supporting the software's development team is essential.\\nPlease consider buying a subscription.\" 10 58\n    msg_info \"Disabling subscription nag\"\n    mkdir -p /usr/local/bin\n    cat <<'EOF' >/usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh\n#!/bin/sh\n# PDM ships its web UI as a Yew/WASM app in a single JS bundle - there is no\n# proxmoxlib.js and no HTML template to patch, so the dialog is confirmed at\n# runtime instead. The nag gates the update refresh: its on_close callback is\n# what sends RefreshAll, so it must be close()d, never removed from the DOM.\n# Re-applied by the apt hook after every package update.\nBUNDLE=/usr/share/javascript/proxmox-datacenter-manager/js/pdm-ui_bundle.js\nif [ -s \"$BUNDLE\" ] && ! grep -q PDM_NO_MORE_NAGGING \"$BUNDLE\"; then\n    echo \"Patching PDM subscription nag...\"\n    cat >>\"$BUNDLE\" <<'JS'\n\n// PDM_NO_MORE_NAGGING\n(function () {\n  var MARK = 'data-pdm-nag';\n  var dismiss = function (d) {\n    if (d.getAttribute(MARK)) return;\n    d.setAttribute(MARK, '1');\n    var tries = 12;\n    var tick = function () {\n      if (!d.isConnected || tries-- <= 0) return;\n      try { d.close(); } catch (e) {}\n      setTimeout(tick, 120);\n    };\n    requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n  };\n  var scan = function () {\n    document.querySelectorAll('dialog.pwt-outer-dialog').forEach(function (d) {\n      if ((d.textContent || '').indexOf('pdm.proxmox.com') !== -1) dismiss(d);\n    });\n  };\n  var start = function () {\n    scan();\n    new MutationObserver(scan).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n  };\n  if (document.body) { start(); } else {\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start);\n  }\n})();\nJS\nfi\nEOF\n    chmod 755 /usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh\n    /usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh >/dev/null 2>&1\n\n    cat <<'EOF' >/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/no-nag-script\nDPkg::Post-Invoke { \"/usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh\"; };\nEOF\n    chmod 644 /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/no-nag-script\n    msg_ok \"Disabled subscription nag (clear browser cache!)\"\n    ;;\n  no)\n    msg_error \"Selected no to Disabling subscription nag\"\n    rm -f /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/no-nag-script /usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh 2>/dev/null\n    apt --reinstall install proxmox-datacenter-manager-ui &>/dev/null || msg_error \"UI reinstall failed\"\n    ;;\n  esac\n\n  # ---- UPDATE ----\n  CHOICE=$(whiptail --backtitle \"Proxmox VE Helper Scripts\" --title \"UPDATE\" --menu \\\n    \"Update Proxmox Datacenter Manager now?\" 11 58 2 \"yes\" \" \" \"no\" \" \" 3>&2 2>&1 1>&3)\n  case $CHOICE in\n  yes)\n    msg_info \"Updating Proxmox Datacenter Manager (Patience)\"\n    apt update &>/dev/null || msg_error \"apt update failed\"\n    apt -y dist-upgrade &>/dev/null || msg_error \"apt dist-upgrade failed\"\n    msg_ok \"Updated Proxmox Datacenter Manager\"\n    ;;\n  no) msg_error \"Selected no to updating Proxmox Datacenter Manager\" ;;\n  esac\n\n  # ---- REMINDER ----\n  whiptail --backtitle \"Proxmox VE Helper Scripts\" --title \"Post-Install Reminder\" --msgbox \\\n    \"IMPORTANT:\n\nProxmox Datacenter Manager is still under active development. Do not rely on it as the only management path to your nodes.\n\nAfter completing these steps, it is strongly recommended to REBOOT.\n\nAfter the upgrade or post-install routines, always clear your browser cache or perform a hard reload (Ctrl+Shift+R) before using the PDM Web UI to avoid UI display issues.\" 20 80\n\n  # ---- REBOOT ----\n  CHOICE=$(whiptail --backtitle \"Proxmox VE Helper Scripts\" --title \"REBOOT\" --menu \\\n    \"Reboot Proxmox Datacenter Manager now? (recommended)\" 11 58 2 \"yes\" \" \" \"no\" \" \" 3>&2 2>&1 1>&3)\n  case $CHOICE in\n  yes)\n    msg_info \"Rebooting Proxmox Datacenter Manager\"\n    sleep 2\n    msg_ok \"Completed Post Install Routines\"\n    reboot\n    ;;\n  no)\n    msg_error \"Selected no to reboot (Reboot recommended)\"\n    msg_ok \"Completed Post Install Routines\"\n    ;;\n  esac\n}\n\nheader_info\necho -e \"\\nThis script will Perform Post Install Routines.\\n\"\nwhile true; do\n  read -rp \"Start the Proxmox Datacenter Manager Post Install Script (y/n)? \" yn\n  case $yn in\n  [Yy]*) break ;;\n  [Nn]*)\n    clear\n    exit\n    ;;\n  *) echo \"Please answer yes or no.\" ;;\n  esac\ndone\n\nstart_routines", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

### [2026-08-31T13:14:48.188047+00:00] assistant

Done — I removed the PDM subscription popup using the **community-scripts post-pdm-install** approach.

## What I changed

Applied the `post-pdm-install` nag-removal logic from:

```text
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/community-scripts/ProxmoxVE/main/tools/pve/post-pdm-install.sh
```

Specifically, on the PDM container I patched:

```text
/usr/share/javascript/proxmox-datacenter-manager/js/pdm-ui_bundle.js
```

and installed the persistence hook:

```text
/usr/local/bin/pdm-remove-nag.sh
/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/no-nag-script
```

That means the patch should be re-applied automatically after future package updates.

## Verification

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| PDM web root loads | **HTTP 200** |
| PDM JS bundle loads | **HTTP 200** |
| `PDM_NO_MORE_NAGGING` marker present | **Yes** |
| Auto-close logic present | **Yes** |
| PDM API version works | **HTTP 200** |
| PDM resources status works | **HTTP 200** |
| Remote Proxmox cluster still healthy | **0 failed remotes** |

## Important browser step

You may need to do a hard refresh because your browser likely cached the old JS bundle:

```text
Ctrl + Shift + R
```

or clear cache for:

```text
https://10.0.20.181:8443/
```

After that, the popup should auto-close / stop blocking the UI.
